- **Print options + swap macros panels in the Filament Calibration wizard.** The same `PrintOptionsPanel` + `SwapMacrosPanel` from the PrintModal now appear on the calibration preset page, reading your saved per-printer-model preferences (set once via any regular library print, applies everywhere) and upserting on each successful start. `PrintQueueItem` born from a calibration job carries every toggle — `bed_levelling`, `flow_cali`, `layer_inspect`, `timelapse`, `mesh_mode_fast_check`, `gcode_injection`, `execute_swap_macros`, `swap_macro_events` — so the dispatcher fires swap macros (A1 plate-change), bed levelling, and your other per-job choices for a calibration print exactly the same way it does for a regular library print. Calibration-tuned defaults: `bed_levelling=true`, `flow_cali=false` (pre-print flow-cali would override the gcode's M900 K sweep), swap macros opt-in. MQTT-action macros (P1S chamber light on/off etc.) keep firing through `fire_event_macros` on `print_started` / `print_finished` events automatically — no per-job toggle, no calibration-specific gate.

- **Bambu Studio calibration assets mirrored into the repo.** `backend/app/data/calib_assets/` ships with the 12 files BS uses for its own calibration wizard (3MFs / STLs / STEP) — all mirrored verbatim from BS `resources/calib/` with full AGPL-3.0 attribution in `LICENSE.md`. They're scaffold geometry: BS itself runs full slicing against the active filament profile through `Plater::calib_*` / `CalibUtils::*` for every mode (including the ones we initially thought were "pre-sliced"), so the slicing step is unavoidable. Wave 2 of the calibration roadmap wires our slicer sidecar to consume these scaffolds.

- **Per-mode lifecycle for the Filament Calibration wizard (W2 Phase 0).** Every mode now has one of three states — `disabled` / `verification` / `production` — managed in `backend/app/services/calibration_mode_registry.py`. The wizard greys out disabled modes (with a "Not yet implemented in this build" tooltip) and marks verification-state modes with a yellow "Verification" pill; the API enforces the same gate on `POST /printers/{id}/calibration/sessions`. A new `POST /printers/{id}/calibration/slice-only` endpoint runs verification-state modes through the slicer sidecar and returns the sliced `.gcode.3mf` as a download, so operators can validate per-mode output against BS / Orca reference 3MFs before each mode is wired to dispatch real prints. Phase 0 ships every mode `disabled` — subsequent W2 phases flip them mode-by-mode after sign-off.

- **PA Tower lands in verification (W2 Phase 1).** Picking PA Tower in the wizard opens a new "Verification — download sliced 3MF" page whose preset picker mirrors the SliceModal: a Manual / Bundle segmented switch (when bundles exist) with the same 3-tier cloud / local / standard dropdowns and the All / My Presets / Built-in owner filter in Manual mode, or the per-bundle process / filament name dropdowns in Bundle mode. Card-style slicer picker above the preset row, defaulting to the global `preferred_slicer`. Operator fills the sweep parameters (start K, end K, step, layer height, nozzle diameter), clicks **Download sliced 3MF** (or **Download pre-slice 3MF** to inspect the 3MF BamDude hands to the slicer before it goes through). BamDude trims the BS-mirrored `pressure_advance/tower.stl` to the minimum height covering the sweep, injects one `M900 K<value> L1000 M10` per layer at the right Z (matches BS `GCode.cpp:4124-4125`), sets `seam_position=rear` on the tower object, disables wraparound detection in the project settings, packs everything into a Bambu-format 3MF (top-level `<components>` referencing `3D/Objects/object_1.model`, `Metadata/slice_info.config` header, `Metadata/model_settings.config` with `<plate>` + `<model_instance>`), sends the composed 3MF through your slicer sidecar, and returns the resulting `.gcode.3mf` as a download. Open it in Bambu Studio / OrcaSlicer and compare against the same parameters in their own calibration wizard — if the per-Z M900 K-values and total filament used match, PA Tower will flip from `verification` to `production` in a follow-up release. The preset-source / dropdown / slicer-card components used by the SliceModal moved into `frontend/src/components/preset-picker/` so both call sites stay in sync as the picker UX evolves.

### Changed

- **Calibration kebab entries gated on the printer being online and idle.** The "Calibration" and "Filament Calibration" entries on a printer card both kick off flows that physically drive the printer (run a calibration print, send MQTT commands), so opening them while the printer is offline or mid-print can't work. Both entries are now disabled (greyed, with a tooltip) unless the printer is connected and not `RUNNING` / `PAUSE`. The existing permission gates and the `use_slicer_api` gate on Filament Calibration are unchanged — this stacks on top.

- **Archive list view: hover a thumbnail for an enlarged preview.** The list (table) view on the Archives page showed only a 40 px thumbnail with no way to see the print without opening it. Hovering the thumbnail now pops a 192 px preview — the same CSS-only `group-hover` popup the library list view already uses, anchored at the thumbnail's bottom-right so it never covers the row's text.

- **K-profile cache sync hard-prunes profiles deleted on the printer.** `sync_printer_kprofiles_to_cache` previously only ever inserted or updated — a profile deleted on the printer (or via BamDude's own delete) left a stale `filament_calibration` row behind forever. It stayed invisible on the Profiles tab (that list is built from the live printer push) but lingered on the Filament Calibration tab as a phantom "active calibration", and pre-print bind silently no-op'd against it. The sync now treats the printer as the single source of truth: every cache row for the printer that the live-list walk doesn't match is hard-deleted, regardless of `source` — including manual wizard results. Dependents go with it: `kprofile_notes` (m065 notes) are deleted, `spool_k_profile` / `spoolman_k_profile` links are deleted, and `calibration_audit.filament_calibration_id` is nulled (the audit row itself stays). All four are handled explicitly rather than via `ON DELETE` clauses because SQLite runs with `foreign_keys=OFF` — behaviour is now identical on SQLite and PostgreSQL. `calibration_session` rows are deliberately left intact: a calibration's session history survives even when its resulting K-profile is gone. (Edge case: if the printer reports an empty K-profile list the sync still early-returns without pruning — an empty list is ambiguous between "printer has zero profiles" and "list not received yet", and wiping the whole cache on a transient empty read is not worth it.)

- **Filament Calibration + History kebab entries gated on the "Server-side slicing" master toggle.** When `use_slicer_api` is off in Settings → General → General, both entries are hidden on every printer card — every calibration mode (including PA Pattern, Flow Rate, Auto PA / Auto Flow Rate) needs the slicer pipeline once you cross the BS-parity threshold, so showing entry points that can't function would be misleading. Direct API calls slipping through hit a server-side 409 `{detail: "slicer_sidecar_required"}` from `start_calibration`. `resolve_asset_path()` → `resolve_asset()` returns a `CalibAsset(path, kind)` pair pointing at the BS-mirrored scaffold.

### Fixed

- **Pre-print K-profile bind now fires for external-spool prints (every printer model with external).** `_apply_calibrations_for_print` walked `state.raw_data.ams` only — so `extrusion_cali_sel` never went out before `project_file` on no-AMS printers (A1 Mini / A1 / P1S / X1C without AMS), and even on AMS-equipped printers any operator-initiated mid-job swap to the external holder ran with whatever K the printer had cached from the last AMS slot. The dispatcher now walks `state.raw_data.vt_tray` after the AMS loop on every print, regardless of AMS presence (the two are always co-existing slot pools): `vt.id=254` → external slot 0 (single-external), `vt.id=255` → external slot 1 (H2D dual-external second slot). Per populated vt entry: resolves `filament_id` from `vt_tray[*].tray_info_idx` (or the spool's preset when one is RFID-linked / Spoolman-linked), looks up `spool_assignment(printer_id, ams_id=255, tray_id=slot)`, and calls `apply_active_calibration_to_slot(ams_id=255, slot_id=slot, ...)` — the MQTT layer remaps to the wire IDs (`mqtt_ams_id=255, mqtt_tray_id=255` for single-external per BS DevDefs.h `VIRTUAL_TRAY_MAIN_ID`). Same fallback chain as the AMS path (`spool_k_profile` link → per-`(filament,nozzle,vol,extruder)` active row → no-op when nothing matches); `ams_mapping`-based filtering doesn't apply to externals (the no-AMS branch in `bambu_mqtt` remaps `ams_mapping=[0]` as a firmware placeholder — no external-slot signal there to filter on).

- **K-profile cache sync now updates in place on K-value / name change.** The match key in `sync_printer_kprofiles_to_cache` used to include `name` + `pa_k_value` — so re-tuning the K for the same `(printer, filament, nozzle, vol_type, extruder)` combo (operator re-runs the wizard, or edits the slot from the Profiles UI) was treated as a brand-new row instead of an update of the existing one. The cache ended up with a per-K-value history fan-out per combo, and any subsequent sync that found 2+ matching rows crashed with `Multiple rows were found when one or none was required` (the route handler caught it as a warning, but the cache then drifted from the live printer). The match key is now combo-only: `(printer_id, filament_id, nozzle_diameter, nozzle_volume_type, extruder_id)`. `name` and `pa_k_value` are mirrored from the printer onto the matched row alongside `cali_idx` / `nozzle_id` / `filament_setting_id`. Query uses `LIMIT 1` + `ORDER BY is_active DESC, id DESC` so legacy fan-out data (rows from the old key-shape era) resolves deterministically — `is_active=True` wins, then newest by id; no rows are deleted inline.

- **Calibration archive now correctly links back to the session.** `PrintArchive.calibration_session_id` was always NULL because `enqueue_calibration_print` created the queue item before the session row existed, and the back-reference was never written. The wizard's resume flow relied on this link to find the surviving archive after `on_print_complete` auto-deleted the queue item — operators stuck on "Calibration in progress…" had no way out. Now the order of creation is atomic: session row first (with `print_queue_item_id=NULL`) → queue item born with `calibration_session_id=session.id` already set → session updated with the queue-item id. Scheduler sees a fully linked item at any polling moment.

- **Calibration nozzle temperature now resolves through the cloud preset's inheritance chain.** Bambu cloud presets return only the delta against `base_id`, not the flattened config — `nozzle_temperature` is usually inherited from the base-material parent (`Generic PETG @BBL A1M`), absent or marked `"nil"` on the child. The wizard's hidden auto-fill silently landed on the FE default `nozzleTemp=220`, and `extrusion_cali_set` then wrote 220 °C into the printer's K-profile for PETG/ABS/PA filaments. `get_filament_info` now walks `data.base_id` up the chain (cap 5 hops), parses `"nil"` as "inherit from parent", and falls back to the first concrete numeric. Applies to all three derived fields (`nozzle_temperature`, `hot_plate_temp`, `filament_max_volumetric_speed`).

- **Calibration wizard manual-save dialog text rewritten for PA Tower.** Old shared dialog said "Look at the printed tower. Count lines bottom-to-top" — wrong for PA Tower (which has no drawn lines, only K-band stripes from per-layer M900 changes). Now per-mode: PA Tower gets a `measured_height_mm` number input + Orca-wiki formula `K = Start + (Step × measured_height_mm)`, PA Line / PA Pattern keep their "pick the cleanest line/corner" picker.

- **Calibration wizard "Start" button hover + pending state.** Slicer-sidecar slice + FTP + queue-insert takes 3-10 s; the button had no hover style, no spinner, no disabled-while-pending guard, so operators thought their click didn't register and double-tapped. Now: Tailwind hover/active states with smooth `transition-colors`, local `isStarting` flag blocks re-entry, spinner + "Starting…" label while the start mutation is in flight.

- **`reconcile_session_status` no longer hits MissingGreenlet after the lazy flip.** SQLAlchemy `db.commit()` expires attributes by default; the route immediately validated the session via pydantic, which triggered a lazy-load of `updated_at` outside the greenlet context. `await db.refresh(session)` after the reconcile commit reloads attributes synchronously.

---

## [0.4.5b2] - 2026-05-13

Hotfix on top of 0.4.5b1. Image: `ghcr.io/kainpl/bamdude:0.4.5b2` / `kainpl/bamdude:0.4.5b2`. Pin the exact tag — `:latest` still tracks 0.4.4.1.

### Fixed

- **Fresh installs of 0.4.5b1 failed to boot with `no such column: printer_model`.** `Base.metadata.create_all()` (which runs before the migration chain) creates `filament_calibration` in its post-m063 shape on a brand-new DB — m062 then tries `CREATE INDEX ON filament_calibration(printer_model, ...)` and SQLite raises. Existing installs (where m062 originally created the table with `printer_model`) were unaffected since the column was still there at that point in the chain. m062's two index-create statements are now guarded by `column_exists(printer_model)` — they no-op on fresh installs and m063 lays down the post-rename indexes a moment later. Same shape verified against m063 / m064 / m065 (already idempotent).

---

## [0.4.5b1] - 2026-05-13

First beta of the 0.4.5 cycle — a focused Filament Calibration / K-profile pass plus two adjacent dialog ports (AMS Settings, Printer Settings) from Bambu Studio. Image: `ghcr.io/kainpl/bamdude:0.4.5b1` / `kainpl/bamdude:0.4.5b1`. Pin the exact tag — `:latest` still tracks 0.4.4.1.

### Added

- **Filament Calibration wizard (Bambu Studio parity).** Kebab → Filament Calibration on each printer card opens a guided modal: **Pressure Advance** (PA Line manual 50-line tower, PA Pattern, PA Tower, Auto PA on lidar-equipped X1 / X1E / H2D Pro), **Flow Rate** (9-block coarse → 7-block fine, plus Auto on X1 / H2D Pro), **Towers** (Temperature / Volumetric Speed / VFA / Retraction — print-and-finish, read by eye, enter in slicer). Results saved per `(printer_id, filament_id, nozzle_diameter, nozzle_volume_type, extruder_id)`; active row auto-binds via `extrusion_cali_sel`. Sibling kebab entry opens a History modal listing BamDude rows next to the printer's 16-slot table with refresh-from-printer. H2D dual-extruder: per-extruder tabs, manual one-at-a-time, auto batches both. Backend: `filament_calibration` / `calibration_session` / `calibration_audit` tables (m062), 4 MQTT publishers, 14 REST endpoints, concurrent-session guard, full audit trail, WS `calibration.*` events. Ships with empty `backend/app/data/calib_assets/` — copy BS `resources/calib/` 3MFs there before running manual + auto paths.

- **AMS Settings dialog (Bambu Studio parity).** Gear icon on the AMS panel header opens a per-printer modal: insertion / power-on RFID auto-read, remaining-capacity estimation, AMS filament backup, air-print detection (A1), calibrate AMS (`M620 C<id>`), A1 firmware LITE ↔ FULL, H2D connected-AMS sequence reset. Visibility gated per model; every applied change written to `ams_setting_audit` (m060). en + uk strings sourced from BS; some uk msgstr are still empty upstream and stay English with `// TBD-uk` markers.

- **Printer Settings dialog.** Kebab → Printer Settings opens a tabbed modal (Print Options + Printer Parts) mirroring ~15 BS toggles (AI detections with sensitivity, filament tangle, nozzle blob, FOD check, displacement, open-door check, air purify, auto recovery, prompt sound, snapshot, plate detect / align). Read-only nozzle info on Parts. Per-model `supports.*` hides unsupported rows; audited to `printer_setting_audit` (m061).

### Changed

- **K-profiles bind on every print start.** `background_dispatch` resolves the active calibration per used AMS slot and fires `extrusion_cali_sel` against the printer's LIVE `cali_idx` — re-matched by stable identity (`name` + `filament_id` + `pa_k_value`), not the cached number, since slots reorder when a neighbour is deleted. Closes silent-drift after RFID re-taps / slot reassignments / restarts. Six call sites (RFID refresh, tray-tag drift, auto-spool tagger, inventory + Spoolman slot-assign) collapsed onto one helper.

- **Calibration cache per-printer-instance, not per-model (m063).** `filament_calibration` rows keyed by `printer_id`. Two X1Cs in a farm carry independent K values for the same material; the History modal scopes to that specific printer.

- **Cache mirrors the printer's live K-profile list, event-driven.** Sync runs on every MQTT (re)connect and whenever the printer's list actually changes (hash-diff filtered so it doesn't hammer the DB on every `push_status`). New rows arrive `is_active=false` — you still explicitly promote one row per `(material, nozzle, extruder)` combo. UI fetches stay consistent; you don't have to open the manage dialog for BamDude to catch up after reconnect / on-printer save.

- **Cache row carries printer-side `nozzle_id`.** Every row stores the raw 4-char identifier (`HS00-0.4`, `HH00-0.6`, …). P1S / A1 / A1 mini don't ship a per-profile value — BamDude derives it from the live nozzle hardware state so the column is filled regardless of model.

- **K-profile notes survive printer restarts (m065).** Notes re-keyed to BamDude's stable `filament_calibration.id`. The old `setting_id` keying drifted across restarts and silently re-attached notes to the wrong profile. Existing notes are cleared as part of m065 — clean slate beats fuzzy recovery on data that was already unreliable.

- **Spool ↔ K-profile thin link rows (m064).** `spool_k_profile` + `spoolman_k_profile` drop their copy of `k_value` / `name` / `cali_idx` / `setting_id` / `nozzle_type` / `nozzle_diameter` in favour of a single `filament_calibration_id` FK. One hundred PETG spools sharing the same calibration → one cache row + 100 links instead of 100 duplicated K rows. PA tab UI unchanged.

- **`calibrated_on_printer_id` dropped.** After m063 keyed `filament_calibration` on `printer_id` the column was always equal to it — pure duplicated state. Audit code now reads `printer_id` directly; printer-sync rows stamp `calibrated_by_user_id` with the first admin user id as the "came from printer, not wizard" marker.

- **Archive filter is now `All / Test / Regular` keyed on `is_calibration`.** The old `All files / Sliced GCODE / Source only` filter was meaningful when archive held arbitrary uploads — but archive has been print-history-only since 0.4.2. Query param renamed `file_type` → `kind ∈ {all, calibration, regular}`; localStorage key migrated.

### Fixed

- **"Printer Parts" tab shows nozzle hardware on P1S / A1 / A1 mini.** Those models push the nozzle as a canonical long name (`stainless_steel`, `hardened_steel`, …); our parser only understood the X1-family 4-char code (`HS00`, …) and rendered the long string raw with an empty Flow row. Parser is now a one-to-one port of BS `DevNozzleSystem::s_parse_nozzle_type` — long names (six categories: stainless / hardened / tungsten carbide / brass / E3D / undefined), 4-char codes, and the literal `"N/A"` sentinel. Flow class (`standard` / `high_flow` / `tpu_high_flow`) stored separately on `NozzleInfo` and surfaced on `/printers/{id}/status`.

- **Configure-AMS-slot dialog matches Generic PETG (and other Generic profiles).** Match was by name substring — works for "PLA Basic" but breaks for "Generic PETG" because the cloud-preset name lacks the literal "GENERIC". Match is now by `filament_id` equality with `GFS*` cloud ↔ `GF*` slot normalisation; name substring kept as a legacy fallback.

- **K-profiles edit dialog showed wrong flow type.** Three off-by-one defaults (`'HH00'` instead of `'HS00'` + an inverted ternary) made every existing row open as "High Flow". Existing rows now open with the flow type they were saved with.

- **Single-external slot binds K-profiles correctly.** External tray ID was `254`; BS standard is `255` (`VIRTUAL_TRAY_MAIN_ID`). With `254` the printer silently dropped every `extrusion_cali_sel` aimed at the single-external slot.

- **K-profile row listing on dual-extruder machines.** Handler read non-existent `kp.extruder_id` (correct field is `kp.extruder`) — extruder column was empty for every row.

- **Manual PA wizard captures the printer's assigned `cali_idx` on save.** Wizard now round-trips `extrusion_cali_get` after save_result and stashes the live slot index, so the very next print binds without waiting for an unprompted firmware push.

- **Sync mirrors `filament_setting_id` from the printer.** The printer regenerates `setting_id` on every `extrusion_cali_set`; sync used to update only `cali_idx` and `nozzle_id`, leaving DB drift untouched even when the live value differed. Now mirrored whenever the printer reports a non-empty value that differs from cache.

- **Edit K-profile "Save" with no printer-relevant change no longer hits the printer.** Saving only a note — or clicking Save without changing anything — used to fire `extrusion_cali_set` and force the printer to regenerate `setting_id`. The form now diffs `name` / `k_value` / `filament_id` / `nozzle_id` / `nozzle_diameter` against the loaded profile; identical → note saved locally, no MQTT publish.

- **`/printers/{id}/kprofiles/` writes audited.** Every add / edit / batch-add / delete writes a `calibration_audit` row (`action ∈ kprofile_{add, edit, batch_add, delete}`) with full payload + user id + success / error. Closes the trace gap where mutations from the K-profiles UI page were invisible to forensic queries.

- **AMS humidity / temperature history modal** — missing uk i18n keys + dialog now follows the active theme background variant (warm / cool / oled / slate / forest).

## [0.4.4.1] - 2026-05-12

Hotfix on top of 0.4.4. Image: `ghcr.io/kainpl/bamdude:0.4.4.1` / `kainpl/bamdude:0.4.4.1` (`:latest` now tracks this).

### Fixed

- **Upgrades from pre-m038 installs (e.g. 0.4.0.1) crashed at boot with `no such column: print_archives.bed_type`.** Migration m038's plate-index re-parse seed loaded the full `PrintArchive` ORM entity, which compiled to a `SELECT` that included `bed_type` — a column only added later by m057. On a DB that hadn't yet applied m038, the seed ran before m057 had a chance to create the column and `_run_pending` died mid-chain. Seed now reads + writes named columns explicitly. Same shape preemptively applied to m046 / m057 / m059 so any future column addition can't reproduce the trap.

---

## [0.4.4] - 2026-05-12

Stable 0.4.4 release — consolidates the cumulative b1+b2+b3 beta cycle (see the `[0.4.4b3]` section below for the full detail) plus the post-b3 upstream Bambuddy v0.2.4 audit closeout. Image: `ghcr.io/kainpl/bamdude:0.4.4` / `kainpl/bamdude:0.4.4` (`:latest` now tracks this).

The major user-facing arrivals this release: **Stock forecasting + Logistics view** for the inventory page, **MakerWorld** moves from one-shot import to a managed library (History tab, Re-download, per-variant dedupe with View-in-Library, full meta + local covers), **Maintenance page reshape** (3-column grid, in-header toolbar with Location grouping + Status segmented tabs), **daily log rotation** with operator-managed retention, and the Telegram bot becomes resilient to token swaps and mid-print MarkdownV2 quirks.

### Added

- **Stock forecasting + Logistics view in the inventory (#1184).** Third inventory tab next to Table / Cards. Per-SKU reorder intelligence on top of the real `spool_usage_history` — exponentially-weighted daily rate (30-day half-life), 95%-service-level safety stock, days-left projection, reorder-by date, top-5 stacked chart with ROP lines. Inline editors for lead-time + safety-margin (days|grams) + alert-snooze. Companion **Shopping List** panel walks `pending → purchased → received`; mark-as-received auto-creates `category='Stock'` spools. New global `forecast_global_lead_time_days` setting acts as a floor across SKUs. Two new permissions (`inventory:forecast_read` / `:forecast_write`) back-filled onto existing groups by m059. Hidden in Spoolman mode. Adapted from upstream `37c9d5f2`.

- **MakerWorld "History" tab.** Server-paginated 4-column grid (`GET /makerworld/imports?page=…&search=…&sort_by=…`) with locally-cached cover thumbnails, sliced-for badges, debounced search across filename + title + author. Page-size + sort persist in localStorage.

- **MakerWorld "Re-download" action for already-imported variants.** Replaces the Import button when a variant is in your library; refreshes the on-disk 3MF bytes + meta + cover images while keeping the `library_file_id` stable (so queue items / project links / archives stay attached). New endpoint `POST /makerworld/imports/{id}/redownload`.

- **MakerWorld resolve surfaces per-variant dedupe.** `POST /makerworld/resolve` now returns `already_imported_by_profile_id` — each instance card on the resolve preview surfaces an "already imported" badge + View-in-Library deep-link before you click Import.

- **MakerWorld import metadata persists alongside library files.** New `library_file_makerworld_meta` child table captures title, description, author, license, variant URL, sliced-for list, AMS requirement, material count, raw design/instance JSON. Cover images downloaded locally at import — UI never re-hotlinks MakerWorld's CDN. Migration m056 back-fills historical imports best-effort.

- **Build-plate icon on archive cards + uniform printer/model line (#1253).** Every archive card and list row renders an OrcaSlicer-style bed icon with the full plate name (Cool Plate / SuperTack / Engineering / High Temp / Textured PEI / Smooth PEI) in the tooltip. New `bed_type` column on `print_archives` (m057) with automatic back-fill from on-disk 3MFs. The legacy `Sliced for X1C` prefix is gone — cards now scan as `<name-or-model> [bed-icon] GCODE <hash>` regardless of provenance. Adapted from upstream `79d54a8d`.

- **Copy Spool action on the inventory page (#1246).** Copy button on every spool row (table / cards / grouped) opens the form pre-filled with the source data as a new create. `weight_used` resets to 0; the new row gets a fresh id; the source row is untouched. Adapted from upstream `6a130c09`.

- **5th printable spool-label template (40 × 30 mm) + hex code + bolder brand (#809 follow-up).** Adds a 40×30 single-label-per-page format (common DK / Brother roll size between AMS-holder and box label). Every label now also carries the colour hex code; brand line bumped to **Helvetica-Bold** for arm's-length legibility. Adapted from upstream `83a83ed7`.

- **Defensive `settings` dedupe + UNIQUE(key) index for legacy DBs (m058).** Safety net for operators upgrading from pre-fork Bambuddy SQLite files that lacked the UNIQUE constraint and accumulated duplicate rows over restarts. Fresh installs / clean DBs no-op out. Adapted from upstream `b99ceb26`.

### Changed

- **Cancel-during-dispatch pauses the queue instead of failing it.** Cancelled item flips to `cancelled` and the queue transitions to `paused`; cancelled items appear in the queue-card "Issues" section with a **Restart** button that puts them back into `pending`.

- **Archive + Inventory page-size selectors aligned at `12 / 24 / 48 / 96 / All`.** Archive adds the `All` option (server drops `LIMIT`/`OFFSET` entirely). Inventory default moves from 30 to 24; legacy `15/30/50/100` persisted sizes fall back to 24.

- **Sidebar drag is now group-constrained.** Items only re-order within their own group (Operations / Workshop / Resources / Care / System / Links); cross-group drops are rejected. Whole groups can be reordered by dragging the group header.

- **External links belong to a sidebar group.** New `nav_group` dropdown in `AddExternalLinkModal` (m055). Default for new links is `external`, and the `external` bucket now slots **before** `system` so new links are visible at a glance.

- **Page-title icons match the sidebar.** Archives / Inventory / Stats / Projects / Files / Profiles / System Information / Settings now render the matching lucide icon (green, 24×24) — in line with Printers / Queue / Maintenance.

- **MakerWorld page restyled to match the rest of the app.** Sidebar-style header icon, full-width container, "uses community API" disclaimer moved next to the URL input. Page now splits into **Import** + **History** underline-tabs (persisted in localStorage). URL input has an inline X-clear button.

### Security

- **Bumped pip to ≥26.1 in the production Dockerfile (CVE-2026-6357).** `python:3.13-slim` ships pip `26.0.1` — vulnerable to a self-update-check-after-install attack. We now upgrade pip before installing `requirements.txt`. Adapted from upstream `1c778e8a`.

### Fixed

- **Telegram bot survives token change after a failed start.** When `Bot.get_me()` raised (invalid token / network blip), the bot ended up permanently dead until full app restart — the just-attached module-level routers were never detached, so the next start crashed with `"Router is already attached to <Dispatcher>"`. The error path now detaches routers + closes the session. Two regression tests pin failure-then-success and happy restart paths. Reported in the field.

- **Telegram bot: Printers / `/status` no longer crash when a printer is mid-print.** Float `progress` was interpolated raw into MarkdownV2 message text — `f"{25.0}%"` carries an unescaped `.`. Both call sites cast to `int()` (Bambu reports `mc_percent` as an integer anyway). Three regression tests walk the rendered text. Reported by Vladyslav Biletskyi.

- **Telegram bot auth-middleware: alert popups no longer show literal backslashes.** `_reply` helper now escapes only on the parsed Message path, not the plain-text CallbackQuery alert popup.

- **Spool assign defers MQTT for an empty AMS slot and replays on physical insert (#1247).** Pre-load workflow (weigh-then-assign) used to fire `ams_filament_setting` against an empty slot — Bambu firmware silently drops it and the slicer kept showing default-PLA forever. The endpoint now detects empty slots and skips MQTT; `on_ams_change` replays the full configuration when the slot transitions to loaded (`state == 11`, works for 3rd-party tags without RFID too). Toast now reads "Spool assigned. The slot will be configured when you insert the filament." Adapted from upstream `b42aaca5`.

- **Spool form Slicer Preset dropdown shows every per-printer / per-nozzle variant (#1248).** Cloud presets no longer short-circuit local-presets when logged into Bambu Cloud; per-printer variants (`@P1S` / `@X1C` / `@A1`) no longer collapse into one row. Adapted from upstream `30fe88a3`.

- **In-app updater on native installs: pip + git + npm now run in the app dir, not data dir.** Updater used `settings.base_dir` (= data dir, mounted volume) for `cwd`; native installs with `DATA_DIR` set hit `No such file: requirements.txt`. Switched to the newly-exposed `settings.app_dir` (code-tree path). `_perform_update` also preserves existing SSH origins instead of clobbering them to HTTPS. Adapted from upstream `a85855f2` + `cc5692a2`.

- **Forward-looking HA-addon detection wired in (#1167).** BamDude does **not** ship as a Home Assistant Supervisor addon today — but the update UI now defers to HA Supervisor instead of misleading the operator with a docker-compose snippet if a third party packages BamDude that way. Detection via `SUPERVISOR_TOKEN`; new `update_method='ha_addon'`. Adapted from upstream `4aea4be2`.

- **HA-entity toggle on the printer card now opens a confirmation modal (#1260).** Smart plugs with `Show on Printer Card` enabled used to fire on the first click — operators accidentally toggled things during prints. Fire-once `script.*` entities keep instant behaviour; `switch.*` / `light.*` open a danger-style ConfirmModal. Adapted from upstream `dfd9fced`.

- **Archive card buttons no longer show truncated `"Re..."` / `"Sc..."` labels (#1249).** Label breakpoint moved to `xl:` (≥ 1280 px); below that, buttons render icon-only with `title=` tooltip. Adapted from upstream `a1931455`.

- **Dual-nozzle X2D / H2D / X2 Pro without AMS lost external-spool extruder routing (#1257).** Dual-nozzle detection now prefers the hardware-reported `nozzles[1].nozzle_diameter` over `ams_extruder_map` so dual-nozzle printers running with two external spools (no AMS) route correctly. Adapted from upstream `080176c6`.

- **Non-ASCII filenames no longer crash file-download responses (#1245).** New `build_content_disposition` helper emits both legacy ASCII `filename="..."` and RFC 5987 `filename*=UTF-8''<percent-encoded>` so Chinese / Japanese / Arabic / accented Latin filenames round-trip through Save-As intact. Applied across archives / printer files / project ZIP / labels / maintenance / support bundle. Adapted from upstream `3f58fc74`.

- **Gitea / Forgejo backup failures surfaced as cryptic one-word messages (#1224, #1225, #1239, #1255).** Push paths in all four providers (GitHub, Gitea, Forgejo, base) now extract JSON SHAs through a typed helper, refuse to commit on truncated tree listings, guard against branch-create retry loops, and include HTTP status + body excerpt in every failure. Forgejo v15+ pre-flights `GET /user` so bad-token / no-scope is told apart from inaccessible-repo. 103-case unit suite covers all four backends. Adapted from upstream `7afb303f` + companion PRs.

- **MakerWorld cover images failed to render with "auth required".** Cover endpoints renamed so the substring `/cover` matches the auth-middleware public whitelist (`<img src>` can't carry an Authorization header). JSON `.../meta` endpoint keeps its permission gate.

- **Cancel during reprint dispatch left a zombie "printing" archive.** The reprint path now mirrors the library-file path and marks the archive `cancelled` on `DispatchJobCancelled`.

- **Patched-3MF temp dir was leaked into `/tmp` on cancel mid-upload.** Cleanup moved from the success path to a `finally:` block.

- **Information page → Database → Filaments was blank.** `/api/v1/system/info` now returns the count under `filaments`, narrowed to active spools (`archived_at IS NULL`).

- **MakerWorld "Recent imports" panel didn't refresh after import.** `importMutation.onSuccess` now invalidates the `makerworld-recent-imports` query.

---

## [0.4.4b3] - 2026-05-11

Third beta of the 0.4.4 cycle. Image: `ghcr.io/kainpl/bamdude:0.4.4b3` / `kainpl/bamdude:0.4.4b3`. Pin the exact tag — `:latest` still tracks 0.4.3.

Cumulative since 0.4.3 (covers b1 + b2 + b3): Spoolman inventory becomes a first-class operator experience, .bbscfg slicer preset bundles, MFA at-rest encryption default-on via auto-bootstrap, pause/resume notification events with classified reasons across all transports + frontend visualisation, in-app bug-report bubble (relay-driven GitHub issue creation), STL/OBJ shaded thumbnails, daily log rotation + archive UI, 3D viewer Export-as-PNG, mouse-pick selection + S→popup expand on Printers, Queue page reshape into in-header toolbar with Cards/List/Timeline modes + Hide-offline, Maintenance page reshape (3-col grid + Розташування grouping + Status/History/Settings as segmented tabs), Information sidebar entry promoted from a footer icon. Many fixes from upstream Bambuddy v0.2.4 audit cycle. Full detail below.

### Added

- **Printers page: S-card maximize button opens an M-size card in a centered popup.** Compact view drops the temperature panel, clear-plate button, and per-AMS slot detail for grid density; previously reaching those meant flipping the whole grid to M/L. New Maximize2 button between selection checkbox and kebab opens a fixed-overlay popup with the printer re-mounted as `viewMode='expanded' cardSize={2}`. Closes on backdrop click, Escape, or clicking another printer's expand button. Hidden in M/L/XL.

- **Printers page mouse-pick selection: always-visible checkbox + Ctrl/Cmd-click + Shift-range-select.** Pre-fix the selection checkbox only rendered when at least one printer was already selected, so picking 3-of-20 required Select-All + 17 deselects. Card header now carries an always-visible checkbox; Ctrl/Cmd-click on the card body toggles; Shift-click range-selects in the visible sorted/filtered order. New "Printers" section in KeyboardShortcutsModal documents all four shortcuts.

- **In-app bug-report bubble — floating Bug button → form + optional debug-logging capture → relay-driven GitHub issue** (upstream commits `058f74a7` + `dc4d77b9` + `57092822`) — drift port from the v0.2.3b1→b2 cycle that was logged "ported" but never actually landed. Floating button on every page; panel collects description + optional email + optional screenshot, optionally records 5 min of debug logs (sanitised: printer names / serials / IPs / access codes / cloud emails redacted), and POSTs to `BUG_REPORT_RELAY_URL` (default `https://bamdude.top/api/bug-report`). Relay holds the PAT and creates the GitHub issue, so BamDude never carries one. New `bug_reports` audit table (migration `m054`), 5/hour rate limit, override or unset `BUG_REPORT_RELAY_URL` to redirect or disable.

- **Spoolman inventory UI — full feature port** (upstream [PR #1241](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/pull/1241), commits `b30a2831` + `ef7fd4fa`) — Spoolman was previously notification + raw-sync only; now it's a first-class inventory experience. New tables `spoolman_slot_assignments` (Spoolman spool per (printer, ams, tray)) and `spoolman_k_profile` (per spool/printer/extruder/nozzle) via migration `m053`; `spools.storage_location` mirrors Spoolman's location field. 19 new endpoints under `/spoolman/inventory/*`: spool CRUD + bulk-create + archive/restore, slot-assignment CRUD, AMS weight sync, K-profile per-spool save, filament catalog + PATCH. K-profile auto-reapply in `on_ams_change` re-issues `extrusion_cali_sel` when stored K-profile diverges from live `cali_idx`. NFC-tag dedup (`merge_spool_extra` clears stale tag from any other spool first), SSRF-guarded URL validation. PrintersPage hooks Spoolman across all slot kinds; SpoolBuddy-coupled paths skipped (no kiosk hardware in BamDude).

- **Slicer Preset Bundles (.bbscfg) — import once, slice many** (upstream commits `060ba509` + `8a313971` + `7e1105dc` + `a50958e4`) — operators without a Bambu Cloud login hit a long tail of preset-resolution failures (cloud-gated presets, "from User" sentinel rows, dangling `inherits` paths). New flow: upload a BambuStudio Printer Preset Bundle once, then SliceModal picks process + filament names from it directly. Four endpoints under `/api/v1/slicer/bundles` (upload / list / get / delete), `SlicerBundlesPanel` in Settings → Print, deterministic SHA-256 prefix as bundle id (re-upload is idempotent). Sidecar must run the `bamdude/profile-resolver` branch with the bundle endpoints cherry-picked.

- **MFA at-rest encryption now default-on via auto-bootstrap** (upstream [#1219](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1219), commit `90743cfa`) — pre-fix, OIDC `client_secret` and TOTP secrets fell back to plaintext when `MFA_ENCRYPTION_KEY` was unset, with only a startup log warning. Key now resolves env var → `DATA_DIR/.mfa_encryption_key` file → auto-generated (TOCTOU-safe `O_EXCL`, mode 0600), matching the `_get_jwt_secret` precedence pattern. Startup re-encryption migration converts any `fernet:`-less rows in place. New `GET /auth/encryption-status` + `<SecurityStatusCard>` under Settings → Authentication → Security surfaces key source / legacy-row counts / decryption-broken flag. Backup ZIP includes `.mfa_encryption_key` so restore on a fresh host preserves access to encrypted secrets; restore extracts it before the DB swap with ZipSlip path-traversal guard.

- **Frontend pause visualisation: pip warning, inline `<PauseChip>`, live elapsed counter, WS toasts.** PrintersPage compact pip flips to `bg-status-warning` on PAUSE (severe HMS still wins red); new `<PauseChip>` renders `Paused • {reason} · 14m` next to printer name with a 1 s client-side ticker against `status.pause_started_at` (server-stamped, survives F5). `useWebSocket.ts` toasts `print_paused` / `print_resumed` frames and invalidates the printerStatus query.

- **`print_paused` / `print_resumed` rolled out to every settings + editor surface** — follow-up to the pause-event rollout below. Events now appear in the Telegram chat event picker, the template editor groups them under "Print events" (no longer "Other"), and the variables panel shows `{reason}` / `{paused_for}` from `EVENT_VARIABLES` + `SAMPLE_DATA`.

- **`on_print_paused` / `on_print_resumed` notification events across all transports.** BamDude knew about pauses but never fired an application-level event on the RUNNING→PAUSE edge — every transport stayed silent. New state-edge detector in `on_printer_status_change` fires `_handle_pause_edge` / `_handle_resume_edge` on exact transitions (runs before snapshot dedup so identical temps don't suppress). Two new `notification_providers` columns (`on_print_paused`, `on_print_resumed`, both default TRUE), migration `m052`. Templates ship `{reason}` / `{reason_code}` / `{hms_code}` on pause and `{paused_for}` (mm:ss) on resume.

- **Pause-reason normalisation: HMS codes + internal hints map to operator-facing categories.** Firmware fires HMS `0300_8001` ("paused by user") for any MQTT pause command, so internal auto-pauses (plate-detect etc.) would mislabel as user-initiated. New `classify_pause_reason()` resolves to `user` / `filament_runout` / `door_open` / `presence_check` / `file_pause_command` / `ai_first_layer_defect` / `ai_spaghetti` / `foreign_object` / `plate_objects` / `hms_other` / `unknown` (14 HMS codes mapped). Internal triggers can plant a one-shot hint via `set_expected_pause_reason()` that wins over HMS — wired into the plate-detect path so it labels as `plate_objects` instead of `user`.

- **Provider settings UI gained Pause / Resume toggles + badges.** `<AddNotificationModal>` Print Events block adds the two toggles (default ON); `<NotificationProviderCard>` expanded settings has matching rows; compact-card chip strip gains amber `Paused` + lime `Resumed` chips. Per-event ntfy priority list picks up both events automatically.

- **STL / OBJ card thumbnails render as shaded 3D surfaces on a transparent background.** Pre-fix matplotlib's `Poly3DCollection` had no shading, so every face was flat `BAMBU_GREEN` regardless of geometry. Now `shade=True` + `LightSource(315°, 45°)` for Lambertian face-normal shading, transparent figure background so the model floats on either theme, supersample 3× + Lanczos downscale with alpha-bbox crop. View pose moved to isometric `(elev=30, azim=-60)`. OBJ uploads now also go through this path (upload + external-folder scan + batch endpoint gates extended from `.stl` to `(.stl, .obj)`).

- **Daily log rotation with operator-managed retention + on-disk archive download/delete UI.** `RotatingFileHandler` (5 MB × 4) replaced with `TimedRotatingFileHandler` at midnight; rotated archives keep the `.log` extension via a custom `namer` so `logrotate`-style tooling still globs them. Retention configurable in Settings → Data Management (default 7 days, range 1–365); changes propagate to the live handler without restart. New `<LogArchivesPanel>` on the Information page lists archives with per-row Download + two-stage Delete; `LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` env var dropped (settings UI is the single source of truth).

- **3D Model Viewer: Export-as-PNG button** parallel to the G-code viewer's. Captures camera angle / wireframe state / plate / theme background; `WebGLRenderer` constructed with `preserveDrawingBuffer: true` so `toDataURL` returns real pixels post-swap. Filename derives from the modal title + active plate (`MyPart_plate_2.png`).

- **Visual feedback when regenerating a library file's thumbnail.** Pre-fix the mutation fired silently with no indication until the toast arrived. Thumbnail container now shows a translucent backdrop + centered spinner + "Regenerating…" label while in flight; tracked via `singleThumbnailMutation.variables`.

- **Project edit modal pre-fills target plates / parts from the print plan.** Empty inputs auto-fill once on open (operator's manual values never overwritten); empty placeholder shows the plan total; clickable `From plan: N` hint re-applies the current plan total when the plan has shifted. Hint hides when input already matches.

- **Project Detail: "Apply to project" button on Print Plan and BOM totals rows.** One click writes `target_count` (sum of plate copies), `target_parts_count` (`printPlan.totals_objects`), and `budget` (`totals_cost + bom_cost`) onto the project. Both buttons share one mutation; hides when the project already matches all three. Tooltip shows the exact values before the click.

- **In-modal G-code + 3D viewer feature wave: theme sync, OBJ format, dual-handle layer slider, layer-play, travel toggle, wireframe/X-ray, streaming progress, Export-as-PNG.** Closes the UX gaps surfaced when the upstream PrettyGCode swap (`c44b6219`) was vendored then ripped out the same week. (1) Theme sync (live background flip, camera preserved). (2) `OBJLoader` mounted as a third branch (3MF + STL + OBJ). (3) Dual-handle layer slider with `start<end` clamping. (4) Layer-play / pause at 80 ms tick × `playSpeed` (1×/2×/4×/8×). (5) Travel-moves toggle (persisted). (6) Wireframe toggle that traverses the mounted group without rebuilding (orbit camera preserved), persisted. (7) Streaming download progress (`fetch().body.getReader()`) with separate "Parsing G-code…" state. (8) Export-as-PNG with a fresh render before `toDataURL()`.

- **Printable PDF spool labels in 4 sizes** (upstream [#809](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/809), commit `864e5c99`). Per-spool icon button + "Print labels…" header action with multi-select picker. Templates: AMS holder (30×15 mm), Brother box (62×29 mm), Avery L7160 (A4, 21/sheet), Avery 5160 (Letter, 30/sheet). Each label carries colour swatch (with stripes for `extra_colors`), brand, material/subtype, display name (respects the user's Inventory naming template), spool ID, and a QR that deep-links to `/inventory?spool=<id>`. ReportLab + `qrcode`, byte-identical across browsers. Endpoints `POST /inventory/labels` + `POST /spoolman/labels`, capped 500 spools.

- **AMS slot Load / Unload from the printer card** (upstream [#891](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/891), commit `3320c7fd`). The `ams_change_filament` MQTT primitive existed but had no HTTP route or UI — operators had to drill into the slot-detail modal. New `POST /printers/{id}/ams/load?tray_id` + `unload` routes gated on `printers:control`; printer-card AMS-slot popover gains Load / Unload buttons. Hidden during RUNNING. H2D Ext-R (tray 255) uses a separate command shape captured fresh against the H2D.

- **Multi-colour swatch rendering — `dual-color` / `tri-color` / `gradient` / `multicolor` effect types now render visually** (upstream [#1154](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1154) + commit `86b54026`). Pre-fix `InventoryPage::spoolSwatchStyle()` always emitted a flat 135° linear-gradient ignoring `effect_type`; Sparkle / Wood / Marble / Glow / Matte / Silk / Galaxy / Metal overlays never rendered at all. New `filamentSwatchHelpers.ts` with `buildFilamentBackground` ports the post-fix shape: hard-split bars for dual/tri-color, conic-gradient for multicolor, fixed-pixel checkerboard tile, sparkle pattern that reads on card-sized swatches.

- **Per-VP "Force colour match" toggle for the VP auto-queue** (upstream [#1188](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1188), commit `459cfdc5`). Pre-fix the VP `auto_queue` intake only extracted de-duplicated filament types from the 3MF, so a "slot 1 = red, slot 2 = green PLA" job could land on a printer with the right material in the wrong colours. New `virtual_printers.queue_force_color_match` column (migration `m050`); when ON, intake lifts per-slot `(type, color, used_grams)` and pins them as `force_color_match=True` overrides. Drive-by: VP intake was passing a Python list directly to the `Text` column (SQLAlchemy stringified it defeating downstream `json.loads`); now `json.dumps()`.

- **Per-API-key Bambu Cloud opt-in + ownership tracking** (upstream [#1182](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1182), commits `133ec725` + `d8104060`). Pre-fix every `api_keys` row was ownerless; CI / HA / OctoPrint integrations hitting slice via `X-API-Key` had `current_user=None`, so any `cloud:…` preset reference 401'd or fell back to local. Migration `m049` adds `user_id` (stamped at create) + `can_access_cloud` (default False); new `resolve_api_key_cloud_owner` dependency returns the owning user when the key is valid + cloud-flagged + owner active.

- **Gitea + Forgejo backup providers; GitHub + GitLab refactored into a per-backend `git_providers/` package** (upstream [#1160](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/pull/1160) + [#1224](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1224) + [#1225](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1225), commits `e45f9676` + `7ee89b56` + `23380895`). Self-hosted Gitea / Forgejo can now host profile / spool / archive backups directly. Gitea-shape divergences (refs returns a list; empty repos refuse `POST /git/blobs`; `commits/{sha}` wraps tree.sha in Commit schema) all handled. `ForgejoBackend(GiteaBackend)` inherits all fixes. Lands in the post-fix shape — no three-bug regression history.

- **Filament Track Switch (FTS) accessory support — print modal filament dropdown is no longer empty on X2D/H2D with FTS installed** (upstream [#1162](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1162)). With FTS the AMS is no longer wired to a single nozzle, so the per-AMS `info` bitmask reports `0xE` ("uninitialized") on bits 8-11. BamDude's parser skipped 0xE when building `ams_extruder_map`, leaving it empty — and the dropdown's `extruderId === nozzle_id` filter then dropped every slot. New `FilaSwitchState` from `print.device.fila_switch`; when `installed=true` the per-extruder filter is skipped and routed slots get `[L]`/`[R]` badges.

- **Optional per-printer Snapshot URL override for external cameras** (upstream [#1177](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1177) follow-up). go2rtc still emits a warm-up frame on fresh MJPEG connections; the clean solution is the camera's dedicated single-frame endpoint (`/api/frame.jpeg`) when one exists. New `external_camera_snapshot_url` column (migration `m047`); when set, every single-frame capture path (snapshot / notification thumbnails / finish photo / layer timelapse / Obico ML / plate-detect) routes through it. Live-view stays on the stream URL.

- **Non-proxy virtual printers now mirror the live target printer's MQTT state to slicers** (upstream [#1193](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/pull/1193), commit `7dea33d0`). In non-proxy modes the slicer used to see a frozen synthetic idle state — no AMS slot detection, no FTS routing, no per-filament k-profiles, no live camera. New `MQTTBridge` reuses BamDude's existing MQTT subscription to cache real `push_status` + `get_version`; VP's `_send_status_report` serves a near-byte-identical copy with only `gcode_state` / `gcode_file` / `subtask_name` overridden. Identity rewriting at cache time (serial + LE-uint32 IP fields → VP). Other command responses fan out raw to slicers; slicer commands forward to the real printer via `publish_raw` (except `project_file` / `gcode_file` which terminate locally). Camera is a raw TCPProxy on `:322`. Non-proxy now also needs port 322 published.

- **Opt-in iframe embedding via `TRUSTED_FRAME_ORIGINS` env var** (upstream [#1191](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1191), commit `b02350d4`). Default `X-Frame-Options: SAMEORIGIN` + `frame-ancestors 'none'` blocks Home Assistant Webpage panel since HA on `:8123` is a different origin from BamDude on `:8000`. Comma-separated `scheme://host[:port]` list relaxes CSP to `frame-ancestors 'self' <list>` and drops `X-Frame-Options`. Strict validation (no paths / wildcards); invalid entries warn and skip rather than fail boot.

- **OIDC: human-readable usernames + per-provider default group for auto-created users** (upstream [#1173](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1173), commit `d0d0be89`). Pre-fix users without a usable email claim landed with opaque `oidc_<sub30>` usernames. New `_derive_oidc_username_seed` resolves: email local-part → `preferred_username` → `name` → `provider_sub[:30]`, sanitising each candidate. New `oidc_providers.default_group_id` (migration `m051`) lets auto-created users land in a configurable group instead of the hard-coded "Viewers".

### Changed

- **Queue page: in-header responsive toolbar + Hide-offline + Cards/List/Timeline view modes (S compact mode dropped).** Single in-header toolbar (search + status + location + Hide-offline + sort + view-modes) matching the PrintersPage shape; old separate filter-row + 4-mode picker gone. View modes renamed to Cards / List / Timeline with matching icons. Hide-offline filters out queues whose printer is currently disconnected (1:1 queue↔printer mapping). On narrow viewports the inline ribbon collapses to two icon-only overflow menus (Filters / View) via `ResizeObserver`. Existing `queueViewMode='compact'` preferences auto-upgrade to `'expanded'` (Cards) on next load.

- **Maintenance page reshaped to match Printers / Queue header pattern.** Old underline-style tabs + 2-column status grid → single in-header toolbar (Wrench title icon + search + status filter + location filter + Hide-offline + sort + Status/History/Settings segmented control) over a 3-column grid. Status filter narrows printers to Due / Warning / OK / All. Sort options: **Upcoming events** (default — same "issues first" behavior as before, now an explicit choice) / Name / Print hours / Розташування; the Розташування sort also groups cards by location header (mirrors PrintersPage). Hide-offline filters out printers whose connection is down (`printerStatus.connected` from QueryClient cache + WS-driven `statusCacheVersion` re-trigger). Tabs stay visible on narrow viewports as icon-only segmented buttons; Filters + Sort collapse into two overflow icon menus via `ResizeObserver`. Backend: `PrinterMaintenanceOverview` schema gained `printer_location` (populated from `Printer.location`) so the frontend can filter / group / sort without a second `/printers` round-trip.

- **System info promoted from a footer icon to a real sidebar entry "Information / Інформація".** The `/system` route lived only behind an Info icon at the bottom of the sidebar — invisible in the collapsed icon-only variant most operators run by default. New entry in `defaultNavItems` between Notifications and Settings, gated on `system:read`; both bottom-icon NavLinks removed. `LEGACY_GROUPED_ORDER_PRE_SYSTEM` migration auto-upgrades operators on the previous-cycle's grouped default; manual reorders keep their order with `system` appended via the existing add-new-items pass.

- **KeyboardShortcutsModal — every string i18n'd, new Printers section, multi-key combo rendering.** Pre-fix the modal hardcoded English for every category header, footer, "Go to {label}", and per-shortcut description; on a Ukrainian locale it opened as a wall of mixed languages. Every label now flows through `t()` against the new `shortcuts.*` namespace; multi-key combos render as adjacent KeyBadges separated by `+`; new "Printers" section documents the four mouse-pick shortcuts.

- **SliceModal preset-source toggle unified: Manual / Bundle as a single 2-state switch.** Pre-fix the modal had the 3-state owner segmented filter and the bundle dropdown side-by-side; in bundle mode the owner filter was visibly active but had no effect. Now `PresetSourceControl` renders `[Manual | Bundle]` segmented (only when ≥1 `.bbscfg` is imported) with a mode-specific sub-control below. Switching Bundle picks the last-used id from localStorage if still valid, else the first bundle.

- **Card thumbnails switched from `object-cover` to `object-contain`** so 3D-rendered model thumbnails aren't cropped. Pairs with the new STL transparent-background pipeline. Applied across archive + library file thumbnail sites; user-uploaded photos (project covers, MakerWorld product images) keep `object-cover`.

- **Inventory By-Material stat card shows all materials, not just the top 4.** Pre-fix `.slice(0, 4)` silently dropped anything beyond the four heaviest; operators with a typical farm range (PLA + PETG + ABS + ASA + TPU + PC + Wood) saw only the four biggest. Cap removed; `flex-wrap` handles overflow to multiple rows.

- **Inventory stat card order: By Material now sits between Total Inventory and Total Consumed** — puts related "where is the weight?" cards next to each other.

- **Slicer Bundles panel moved to the Print tab + proper en/uk translations.** Pre-fix it lived under General → Profiles → Slicer API where it didn't belong (General is integration config; the bundle list is a print-time pick). Panel-side i18n was using `defaultValue:` English fallbacks even on `language=uk`. New `settings.slicerBundles.*` namespace covers the 14 panel strings.

- **Docker update guidance uses the resolved tag — image-pull and source-build paths shown side by side.** Pre-fix the UI showed a single `docker compose pull && docker compose up -d` snippet that only worked for the stable `:latest` case (pinned tags no-op'd; betas silently fetched a stale stable). Now image-pull block (with stable vs beta hint) + source-build block (`git checkout vX.Y.Z[bN] && docker compose build --pull && up -d`) using the resolved tag.

- **PrintersPage header refactor: PrinterIcon + responsive toolbar with overflow menus on narrow viewports** (upstream [#1203](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/pull/1203), commit `3c0c7a8d`). Pre-fix the 2-row layout wrapped awkwardly at tablet widths and used native `<select>` chrome. Single-row toolbar driven by `ResizeObserver` collapses to three icon-only overflow menus (Filters / View / Actions) when the inline ribbon overflows. Two new themed primitives (`ToolbarDropdown` / `ToolbarMenu`) replace native `<select>`. Kept BamDude's 2-stage bulk-select + separate `<BulkPrinterToolbar>` (upstream's single-toggle shape didn't fit).

- **VP card shows host Tailscale IP + MagicDNS hostname for paste-into-slicer; LE cert provisioning removed as dead code** (upstream `64899a8c`). The Tailscale-LE-cert toggle was supposed to skip CA import in the slicer, but BambuStudio + OrcaSlicer validate MQTT only against the bundled BBL CA store — LE certs reject with "-1" before hostname/IP logic runs. CA import is unavoidable; LE provisioning was dead code (`provision_cert` / `cert_needs_renewal` / `ensure_cert` + `_cert_renewal_loop` + restart plumbing all deleted). VirtualPrinterCard now renders Tailscale IPs + MagicDNS as copy-to-clipboard monospace lines via new `GET /virtual-printers/tailscale-status` endpoint.

### Fixed

- **PrinterInfoModal label drift vs Edit dialog + missing rows for plate-clear and swap-mode.** The read-only info modal used a one-line cleanup label while the edit dialog had a verbose form — same setting, two unrelated-looking labels. Plate-clear and swap-mode rows weren't surfaced in info at all. Both modals now share `printers.modal.cleanupAfterPrintLabel`; the parenthetical hint moved into a separate paragraph in edit. New rows: plate-clear (Enabled/Disabled badge), swap-mode + active profile label (case-insensitive model match against `SWAP_PROFILES`).

- **PrinterInfoModal swap-profile catalog query referenced a non-existent function.** `queryFn: api.getSwapProfiles` — but `getSwapProfiles` lives on `macrosApi`, not `api`. TS missed the typo via `api`'s implicit index signature; data stayed undefined and the row fell through to the raw-id fallback. Switched to `['macros', 'swap-profiles']` + `macrosApi.getSwapProfiles` so the modal shares PrintersPage's warm cache.

- **Slicer Bundle import: cherry-picked the missing endpoints into our orca-slicer-api fork.** `.bbscfg` upload returned 400 because our `bamdude/profile-resolver` branch never had `POST /profiles/bundle` — that lived on `maziggy/orca-slicer-api`'s `bambuddy/bundle-import` branch. Cherry-picked `a3172c5` + `71ab7ea` + `07c5f1a` onto our fork. Operators on the older sidecar must rebuild via `docker compose build --pull --no-cache`.

- **Bundle-import error toast cleaned up — no raw JSON envelope, no "sidecar" jargon.** Pre-fix the toast showed `{"detail":"..."}` verbatim because `importSlicerBundle()` is binary (skips `request<T>()`) and used `response.text()` directly; backend `_format_sidecar_error()` also fell back to raw HTML on Express's default 404 page. Backend now handles 5 JSON shapes + HTML/text fallback with `_strip_html()`; user-visible 4xx prefix is "Slicer API rejected bundle" (was "sidecar"); frontend parses `error.detail` once.

- **FilamentHoverCard now renders the `(Spoolman)` source indicator the test suite asserted on.** The component declared `fillSource?: 'ams' | 'spoolman' | 'inventory'` and tests verified the `(Spoolman)` label, but render never branched on it. Now appears inline next to the fill percent when `fillSource === 'spoolman' && fillLevel !== null`.

- **In-app updater works for pre-release tags — `git reset --hard` targets the explicit tag, not `origin/main`.** Pre-fix the apply step hardcoded `origin/main`, which silently no-op'd betas tagged on `dev`. Apply now accepts an optional `tag_name`; backend resolves it via `_resolve_git_ref()` and runs `git reset --hard refs/tags/<ref>`. Companion fixes: `_is_release_prerelease()` honours both the name suffix (`b`/`beta`/`alpha`/`rc`) and GitHub's `prerelease: true` flag; `/releases` query raised from 20 to 100 to guard against 20+ consecutive betas hiding the stable.

- **m052 seed inserts UA copy directly when `settings.language='uk'`.** Without this, the seed always used English `DEFAULT_TEMPLATES` and pause notifications fired in English on UK installs until `_reconcile_locale_on_startup` caught up. Seed now reads `settings.language` and opens `data/notification_templates_<lang>.json` for the INSERT; falls back to English when the language is unset / unsupported / JSON malformed.

- **m053 migration table-name typo crashed startup: `spools` → `spool`.** The migration created the two new Spoolman tables fine but then tried to add `storage_location` to a non-existent `spools` table — BamDude's `Spool.__tablename__ = "spool"` (singular); upstream uses `spools` and the SQL was copied verbatim. Fix swaps to the singular name; same correction applied to the Postgres `tag_uid` widen path. Idempotent re-run safe.

- **AMS Slot configuration: long preset names un-truncate inline on hover** (upstream [#1237](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1237), commit `20fa8fbf`). Long names like `SUNLU PETG GLOW IN THE DARK GEN2 @Bambu Lab H2C 0.4 nozzle` were clipped, and several near-identical entries differing only in nozzle size were indistinguishable. Native tooltips wait 500-1000 ms; `group-hover:whitespace-normal` + `break-all` reveals the full name immediately on hover. `title=` kept as fallback for AT / touch.

- **Label Picker modal: 4 templates pack into a 2×2 grid + spool list shrinks on tight viewports** (upstream [#1230](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1230), commits `61314cf2` + `4c0a12b9`). On 1080p with browser chrome / DPI scaling, the four stacked templates + footer overflowed `max-h-[90vh]` and clipped the Cancel button. Spool list switched from `min-h-[160px]` to `min-h-0` so it yields height; templates render as `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2` so they pack 2×2 above `sm`, trimming ~150 px vertical.

- **SliceModal Slice button stays disabled until the preview slice resolves** (upstream `a64cfbbd`). The synthetic single-slot fallback let the button enable before the slicer reported real slot consumption; clicking dispatched against opaque defaults and produced a wrong slot map. `filamentReqsQuery.isSuccess` now gates the tier-mode `isReady` chain. Bundle mode untouched (synchronous slot list from filament names).

- **Docker: data-volume ownership normalised at startup via gosu entrypoint** (upstream [#1211](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1211), commit `3407afc0`). Pre-fix the `chmod 777 /app/data` Dockerfile workaround missed runtime-created subdirs (`virtual_printer/uploads`, `certs/`), and dockerd creates bind-mount sources as root — both produced `Permission denied` and "VP doesn't work" reports. New `docker-entrypoint.sh` chowns `/app/data` + `/app/logs` to `PUID:PGID` (gated on a top-level ownership check so restarts skip traversal), drops via `gosu`, then execs the app. Compose template drops the `user:` directive in favour of `PUID`/`PGID` env vars; explicit `user:` / `--user` is detected and bypasses the chown.

- **RFID auto-create resolves shared-hex Bambu Lab catalog rows by material variant** (upstream [#1227](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1227), commit `972e6352`). Three catalog rows share `#FFFFFF` — Jade White (PLA Basic), Ivory White (PLA Matte), White (PLA Silk) — and the lookup filtered on manufacturer + hex only, so the first-inserted (Jade White) won every match. Same bug bit every shared-hex pair across PLA Basic / Matte / Silk. Adds `tray_sub_brands` material filter + explicit `ORDER BY id` for deterministic fallback. Already-mis-named spools don't auto-correct — matcher only fires on new RFID-driven creation; manual rename needed.

- **AMS auto-assign preserves live K-profile cali_idx when no stored K-profile exists** (upstream `b30a2831` from [#1241](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/pull/1241)). `auto_assign_spool` only fired `extrusion_cali_sel` on a stored-K-profile match; without one the slot reverted to slot 0 after every RFID re-tap. New fallback branch reads the slot's live `cali_idx` from AMS state (when present and ≥ 0) and re-issues with that value, so operator-selected calibration sticks.

- **Filament usage double-counted on AMS auto-fallback to a same-material spool** (upstream [#957](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/957), commit `c6e6c4cd`). When a spool ran out and the AMS swapped to a sibling slot, the tracker credited the original with the full 3MF estimate AND added the fallback's remain%-delta — a 78 g print recorded as 138 g across two spools. Two interacting bugs: tray-change recorder gated on literal-string `state in ("RUNNING","PAUSE")` (P2S transitions through LOADING mid-swap), and the usage-tracker splitting branch gated on `not slot_to_tray` (which is set by every print). Recorder now keys on lifecycle flags; splitting branch takes over whenever `tray_change_log` has > 1 entries.

- **Slicer "Send to printer" rejected cached push with "storage needs to be inserted" on P1S/A1 targets** (upstream [#1228](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1228), commit `ceffcfae`). The 0.4.2 cached-as-base bridge mirror passed the live target's `push_status` through with only an IP rewrite; P1S/A1 firmware `01.10.00.00` without SD inserted doesn't report the three storage indicators BambuStudio's Send pre-flight reads, so the slicer never opened FTP. Cached-as-base path now ORs `0x100` (`HAS_SDCARD_NORMAL`) onto `home_flag`, forces `sdcard=True`, and `setdefault`s a `storage: {free, total}` block — real printer values pass through unchanged when present. VPs FTP to BamDude's filesystem so the printer's SD is irrelevant on these paths.

- **Camera popup + deep-route refresh rendered a blank page** (upstream [#1221](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1221), commit `bb03a2b3`). Reverts our earlier `base: ''` fix from v0.2.4b1→b2 (PR #1195). Relative asset URLs broke deep routes: `/camera/<id>` resolved `./assets/index-XXX.js` to `/camera/assets/...`, the SPA catch-all returned `index.html`, and browsers refused to execute it as a module under nosniff. Restored Vite's default `base: '/'`. Path-prefixed reverse-proxy users left as wontfix — full subpath-aware bootstrapping has too many edge cases (PWA scope, push subscriptions, etc.); supported workaround is HA Webpage panel embedding via `TRUSTED_FRAME_ORIGINS`.

- **Settings page entered an infinite PUT loop on open.** The new `log_retention_days` dirty-check compared raw `settings.log_retention_days !== localSettings.log_retention_days` without a `?? 7` nullish fallback, so any transient `undefined` registered as dirty → auto-save → refetch → still dirty → loop. Brought into line with the other retention fields' `(x ?? default) !== (y ?? default)` pattern.

- **3MF "-1" inherit-sentinel sanitiser for sliced MakerWorld packages** (upstream [#1201](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1201)). MakerWorld 3MFs ship `Metadata/project_settings.config` with `"-1"` values that BambuStudio's GUI reads as "inherit from parent" — but the CLI's `StaticPrintConfig` runs *before* `--load-settings` overrides, so the sentinel trips the lower-bound range check (`raft_first_layer_expansion: -1 not in range [0.0, ...]`). Sanitiser drops only allowlisted keys (`raft_first_layer_expansion`, `tree_support_wall_count`, `prime_tower_brim_width`) when their value is exactly `"-1"`, re-zips every other entry byte-identical. Array values + non-allowlisted keys with `-1` left alone.

- **Back-to-back same-3MF prints stamped the second archive with the previous plate's metadata** (upstream [#1204](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1204), commit `713b8538`). On consecutive plates of the same multi-plate 3MF, MQTT `subtask_name` lagged across the boundary while `gcode_file` was fresh; FTP candidate list built subtask-first hit the previous plate's still-resident upload first. The 0.4.3 m038 work fixed only the adoption side. New `peek_plate_index_in_3mf()` validates the downloaded 3MF against `parse_plate_id(filename)`; on mismatch, retries FTP across the 5 path candidates × the swapped-suffix `.gcode.3mf` / `.3mf` variants via new `swap_plate_suffix()` helper.

- **Printer card thumbnail showed the wrong plate on multi-plate 3MFs when firmware dropped the plate path from `gcode_file`** (upstream [#1166](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1166), commit `889c8bd8`). P1S `01.10.00.00` and similar put only the `.3mf` filename in `print.gcode_file`; the cover endpoint's `plate_(\d+)\.gcode` regex never matched and `plate_num` defaulted to 1. Tri-tier resolution: (1) BamDude-dispatched plate via new `dispatched_plate_id` + `dispatched_subtask` on `PrinterState`; (2) `parse_plate_id` on `gcode_file`; (3) 3MF-scan fallback — open the archive and scan for the single `plate_N.gcode` entry. Cache key collapsed since plate_num is now late-bound.

- **Printer Info modal copy buttons now work on plain-HTTP LAN deployments** (upstream [#1174](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1174), commit `59c58362`). `navigator.clipboard.writeText()` requires a secure context; on bare-IP HTTP LAN it's undefined and the icon never flipped to the success tick. Adopts the off-screen-textarea + `execCommand('copy')` fallback pattern other components already use.

- **Project cover photo gains a portal-mounted hover preview at 384×384 px** (upstream [#1155](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1155) follow-up, commit `82a593de`). The 3:1 hero strip used `object-cover` and cropped tall MakerWorld photos into a horizontal sliver. New `ProjectCoverHoverPreview` renders via `createPortal` to escape the card's `overflow-hidden`, with `object-contain`, viewport-edge flipping (right-edge → flip left), and `pointer-events-none` to prevent flicker loops.

- **`formatTimeOnly` tests are locale-agnostic** (upstream [#1213](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1213), commit `02fbebba`). `toLocaleTimeString` separator varies by locale (`"."` on `en_DK`, U+202F narrow no-break space on modern `en_*`, `":"` elsewhere); hard-coded `":"` assertion failed everywhere else. Switched regex to `\D+`.

- **`Plate_1.gcode.3mf` archives landed as `Plate_1.gcode` in UI / on disk + broke timelapse name-matching** (upstream [#1152](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1152), commit `01a7e6ee`). `Path(filename).stem` only drops the last suffix, leaving `Plate_1.gcode`. Three symptoms: archive UI label, archive directory name, and `fetch-timelapse` substring-match against `Plate_1.mp4`. New `resolve_display_stem()` strips `.gcode.3mf` / `.3mf` / `.gcode` case-insensitively in priority order; wired into 4 sites.

- **Swap-mode printers no longer auto-clear the plate or fire the change_table macro after failed/aborted/cancelled prints.** Both swap paths in `on_print_complete` ran independently of terminal status, so a 12-hour print failing at hour 11 had its plate "auto-cleared" and the change_table macro physically rotated the swap rig — jamming on the still-attached part or rotating a fouled plate into the next print. Both branches now gate on `status == "completed"` via a `_swap_status_ok` predicate; non-success drops through to the regular plate-clear-gate path.

- **Pinned the plate-clear gate contract: every terminal status arms the "clear plate" confirmation** (upstream [#1171](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1171), commit `25eab968`). Upstream's pre-fix gate raised only on `("completed", "failed")` — touchscreen-aborts (`aborted`) and cancellations slipped through and the next queued print auto-dispatched onto a fouled bed. BamDude's arm path was already unconditional, so no code change needed. New `TestPlateClearGate` parametrises all four terminal statuses + a defence-in-depth unknown-status case to lock the contract.

- **Print-complete notification "duration" reported the slicer estimate, not actual elapsed time** (upstream [#1198](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1198), commit `a6c53798`). A print cancelled 2 min into a 3 h estimate notified `duration: 3h`. Now computes `actual_time_seconds` from `completed_at - started_at` when both are populated; `{{duration}}` resolves via `actual or estimate`. Companion: `update_archive_status` now also stamps `completed_at` on `status=cancelled` (previously only completed/failed/aborted got a timestamp).

- **Permissions editor displayed several rows in raw English regardless of UI language.** `permissionI18n.ts` splits backend labels like `"Read Api Keys"` into (action, resource) and looks both up; maps missed (a) `.title()`-cased multi-word resources (`Api Keys` / `Kprofiles` / `Makerworld` / `Websocket`) which only had the canonical-cased section-header forms, (b) bucketed resources without their own section header (`users` / `groups` / `git` / `stats` / `ams_history`), (c) the `Purge` / `Import` / `Connect` actions, and (d) multi-word actions `Notes Write` / `Filter By User`. All added to `CATEGORY_KEYS` / `ACTION_KEYS` / `LABEL_OVERRIDES` with new locale keys.

- **File browser didn't refresh after linking a folder to a project.** `updateFolderMutation::onSuccess` invalidated only the folder queries; `library-files` and `project-print-plan` stayed stale until manual reload. Symmetric to the per-file mutation which already invalidated both.

- **Sliced `.gcode.3mf` files were filtered out of the project print plan.** `_is_plan_eligible` matched only `file_type == "3mf"`, but `detect_file_type` collapses `.gcode.3mf` to `"gcode"` — so every sliced file in the library was excluded. Operators saw "0 items" even when the folder linked correctly contained dozens of ready 3MFs. Eligibility now accepts both `"3mf"` and `"gcode"`; recovery migration `m048` SQL updated in lockstep.

- **Files uploaded into a project-tagged folder were missing from the print plan.** Files *moved into* a project folder propagated correctly; files *uploaded into* the same folder bypassed both the M2M pivot inheritance and the plan-row planting. New `inherit_folder_projects()` helper wired into all 5 LibraryFile-creation sites (upload, extract-zip, slice-and-persist, MakerWorld paste, printer FTP-import). Recovery migration `m048` walks `library_file_projects` at startup and backfills missing plan rows; idempotent + preserves operator-curated copies / order_index.

- **Custom groups carrying historically-renamed permission keys could not be saved.** `filaments:*` → `inventory:*` and `github:{backup,restore}` → `git:{backup,restore}` renames left existing `groups.permissions` JSON lists with stale keys; counter showed "101 selected / 95" and Save returned `400 Invalid permissions: filaments:read, …`. New migration `m046_normalize_group_permissions` walks every list at startup: maps known-renamed keys to replacements, drops any unrecognised key, de-duplicates, preserves order. Idempotent.

- **External MJPEG cameras (go2rtc and IP cameras) returned a black warm-up frame** (upstream [#1177](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1177)). `_capture_mjpeg_frame` returned the very first JPEG, but many sources emit a stale keyframe on connection accept (~11 KB pure-black 1280×720, vs 30-45 KB for real content). Critical for Obico AI failure detection (poisons EWMA smoothing), plus broken layer timelapse / finish photo / plate detection / snapshot UX. Fix reads past the first frame and returns the second; falls back to the first on timeout / single-frame streams. Snapshot / rtsp / usb paths and live-view streaming untouched.

- **Backup restore silently lost most data — settings reverted to defaults, most printers and archives missing** (upstream [#1211](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1211); same shape as [#668](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/668)). The DB runs in WAL mode; the original restore used `shutil.copy2()` after `close_all_connections()`, but the route handler's own `Depends(get_db)` session stays checked out across `engine.dispose()` and holds the WAL fd. SQLite's WAL recovery on next `init_db()` re-applied stale fresh-install frames on top of the restored content. Replaced the file copy with SQLite's online backup API (`src_conn.backup(dst_conn)`) — page-by-page protocol routes pages through the destination's own WAL, transaction-isolated from any open session. Postgres path unchanged.

- **MQTT QoS=1 session wedge after ~16-20 cumulative commands** (upstream [#1164](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1164)). paho's default `max_inflight_messages=20` ceiling silently filled on Bambu's broker — `publish()` returned success while packets sat in paho's internal queue. Lifted to 1000 in `connect()`; keeps QoS=1 for cross-model reliability.
- **AMS slot configuration intermittently failed to reach the printer after several configs in a row** (upstream [#1164](https://github.com/maziggy/bambuddy/issues/1164)). The zombie-session watchdog incremented `_ams_cmd_unanswered` on >10 s responses and zeroed `_last_ams_cmd_time`, but the response handler that reset the counter was guarded by `_last_ams_cmd_time > 0` — late responses arriving after the watchdog cleared the timer never reset the counter. Next slow response anywhere later took it to 2 and force-reconnected mid-publish. Fix drops the guard: any `ams_filament_setting` response proves the channel is alive.

### Removed

- **Daily-snapshot release channel removed.** Never used in practice — the `0.5.0b1-daily.YYYYMMDD` tag style never produced a single image. Workflow + helper-script branches gone. Three remaining channels (stable / beta / `:dev` rolling) cover the actual cadence.

### Security

- **`python-multipart` floor raised `>=0.0.26 → >=0.0.27` for CVE-2026-42561** (upstream `a7b3e01b`).
